Static-only follow-up to the 2026-10-20 fast-travel fade-list crash
The owner does not authorize diagnostic playtests. No game run or capture is
required or requested by this investigation. No production code was edited.

Scope: discovered native constructor references, base/clone/stream dispatch,
shared list consumers, field-address candidates and bounded on-disk plugin
references. Individual captures below retain commands, identity and warnings.
Raw displacement/immediate matches are candidate locations, not typed writer
proof or proof of absence. Some exploratory snippets begin mid-instruction;
only verified boundaries and subsequent exact function captures support claims.
Radare2 pdf errors on tail jumps are retained; later pdr/pdf captures resolve
actual jump destinations. No runtime heap contents or write history was added.
This file is not patch authority. The corrupting writer remains unresolved.
